Strict cold tests initially exposed a real render-lifetime bug: shutdown waited
only for the active render, and could free a node before queued previews resumed.
This leaked a suspended function and could leave preview busy forever. Runtime
now counts active AND queued requests, cancels/drains all before node retirement;
normal application close stops new previews and drains owned jobs. A deterministic
active+queued retirement regression passes. Initial failed close/watchdog/debug
logs remain outside checkout; final strict acceptance has no leaks/engine errors.
Tests explicitly finish editor jobs before raw app-free teardown. Tracked old
output logs restored after copying fresh proof. See docs/sequence_shadows.md.

Stage4 now has editable nested scenes/audio, per-instance transform/opacity,
video alpha/chroma and simple2D shadows, with a bounded end-to-end cloud exit.
Masks/glow/general layer tracks, reusable locomotion/native fidelity, measured
long-project cache behavior and owner acceptance still remain independent work.

## 2026-11-05 UTC — optional distance-based stroke smoothing

Advanced Draw now has Stroke smoothing0–32 canvas pixels, default0. A transient
distance-based stabilizer trails the input to reduce jitter; gesture end (including
outside release/focus loss) paints the remaining tail before one existing pixel
Undo. Pressure-zero flushes the LAST positive contact and resets before hover,
so there is no bridge across a pressure gap. Missing pressure creates no cel or
history. Stroke distance/size/color/opacity settings are captured until release.
Pen/eraser pressure and opacity remain separate; fills keep existing behavior.
Only normal RGBA pixels persist; session setting defaults off on cold start.
No new project data/format, online dependency or generated animation cels.

## 2026-11-05 UTC — local reference-sheet viewer and Draw guide

Continuous goal progressed after reusable paths25f60dd (pushed main). Draw now
opens a modeless Reference sheet viewer for a local PNG, with aspect-preserving

side, checked signature/IHDR and dimensions; bounded read also handles source
changes between length check and read. Failed/missing/oversized input keeps the
current sheet. Source bytes are hashed; source changes install only on explicit
Reload. Original PNGs are unchanged. No cloud/AI/external software or source-file
execution. Only2CRLF-preserving app lines wire two small new editor modules.

This first viewer is explicitly session-only and says sheets reopen separately

## 2026-11-07 UTC — isolated background recovery checkpoint serialization

Replaced normal app's synchronous Recovery.checkpoint call with one low-priority
writer. Main capture deep-copies metadata, distinct COW hotImage resources and
explicitly copied immutable cold payload bytes; logical owners and caches never
cross into worker. Root/child cold sharing deduplicates snapshot copies. Worker
adopts into a private320MiBowner/cache context, then uses unchanged integrity,
PNG-budget preflight, atomic storage, journal and source guards. NoNode/GPU access
in worker. Captured context includes session, schedule generation, revision,
actions, lesson signature, frame/view; one writer/preview at a time, one queued
explicit request, no growing snapshot queue. Main polls only completed threads.
Success consumes only captured actions; later edits stay pending/dirty. Oldsession
results do not change New/Open. Exact-current manualSave resets schedulegeneration;
its late obsolete checkpoint is resolved after commit, including at final teardown.
Closing joins owned thread; storage can delay closing. No schema/source/native
asset changes. ManualSave/initialOpen/lesson snapshot serialization remain sync.

driving the existing dropdowns, new StudioTheme icons, F11 fullscreen beside the
existing Settings scale, Simple empty-state hints, fading status toast (status.text is
kept) and plain wording, opt-in dark theme and keyboard navigation in Settings, header
title with unsaved dot. Presentation only; tests/ untouched. Linux xvfb: ui, editor,
pose, world_selection, recovery_write, recovery pass (same as baseline). Windows
verify.py not run. Details, dropped items and runner: docs/REDESIGN-PORT.md.

(164 commits). src/app.gd conflicts resolved keeping main's behaviour: Add scene
uses main's import_actions.add path and filters (pick_scene_file now calls it, so the

(ViewControlsScroll/PhysicsControlsScroll, physics_bar is the scroll strip); empty-state

recovery_write, recovery; scene_view_unit passes. Windows verify.py not run.

## 2026-11-15 UTC — Neon Pro port step 1: Midnight Neon theme foundation (Claude, claude/studio-redesign)

The owner approved a new look: the Iridescent Pro layout with the Midnight Neon theme. Step 1 of 9:
StudioTheme gains `style` (neon default; light = the classic skin unchanged; dark = the classic
re-tone), neon tokens, a precomputed oklch gradient, SVG neon rings, fills and switches rendered at
2x with a size override, a neon make_theme() branch with type variations for later steps, and

"Interface theme" dropdown saved as [display] theme only when changed (an old dark=true still opens
in classic dark). The canvas letterbox and header use neon colours. Layout, visibility and texts
are unchanged. Geist is not bundled: SystemFont fallbacks (Inter on Linux). The selection colour is
pale blue because workspace_controls requires #163854 selected text. Linux xvfb: ui, editor, pose,
world_selection, recovery_write and recovery pass, plus the extra UI smokes (see
docs/REDESIGN-PORT.md). Windows verify.py was not run.

The header is now one 56 px neon bar, following the mock. It holds the brand mark, the project

## 2026-11-21 — Discovery native-close leak fixed

The post-publication goal audit found a real resource leak on browser close
during a native HTTPRequest: nodes freed but Godot retained one resource at
exit. The wrapper now hides/cancels immediately and allows pending service
coroutines to unwind before destruction. Explicit local callbacks run only
after close and still recheck scene/selection/operation context. Studio closing
now refuses new/deferred imports and awaits bridge.finish before Tree quit.
The exact shell integration is three added app.gd lines (two menu hooks plus
one shutdown hook); Renderer/Model/SourceImport/world sources remain untouched.

Actual native --close-live and --quit-live cases both observe a live native
connection, then freed window/service/request with no ScriptError/ObjectDB leak/
resources-still-in-use diagnostics. Focused111headless additions pass, including
28Studio controls per theme, new closing refusal and scene-change-during-close
controls. Earlier109private visual evidence is historical; Root owns the GPU
slot, so no competing graphical run was started. Native probes fetch metadata
only, with no model/image download. The initial close fixture WeakRef inference
annotation failure was corrected before its actual run. Safe evidence is
docs/benchmarks/asset_discovery_shutdown_2026-11-21.json. Updated feature-branch
publication/readback follows; overall goal remains active for primary adoption.
